- **metabarcoding-diversity-data-reproduction**: Use when a source article reports species/OTU occurrence numbers only as Venn-diagram segments (e.g. I-source total 1074: B-I 774, N-I 669, G-I 548; P-source total 1006: B-P 882, N-P 553, G-P 415) and a machine-readable count table is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-diversity-data-reproduction))
- **metabarcoding-diversity-figure-reproduction**: Use when a practitioner needs to reproduce or extend the assessment of how denoising vs. clustering pipeline choices and sequence filtering regimes alter per-sample alpha diversity (Hill Numbers 0/1/2) in amplicon data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-diversity-figure-reproduction))
- **metabarcoding-diversity-table-construction**: Use when 16S rRNA/ITS metabarcoding results for multiple site-source combinations (e.g. inflow I and pond P at farms N, B, G) must be summarized into a machine-readable diversity table with alpha indices and beta-diversity ranks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-diversity-table-construction))
- **metabarcoding-false-positive-diagnosis**: Use when metabarcoding ASV tables from different filtering pipelines (e.g. VTAM vs DALU/OBIbaR) are available and you suspect unfiltered false positives (tag-jumps, contaminants) inflating per-sample richness or deflating inter-sample dissimilarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-false-positive-diagnosis))
- **metabarcoding-family-assignment-aggregation**: Use when per-MOTU metabarcoding results must be collapsed to arthropod family level and compared across primer sets (e.g. mitochondrial 16S rRNA vs COI) or summarized per plant species/mock sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-family-assignment-aggregation))
- **metabarcoding-field-survey-validation**: Use when paired field survey species lists exist for metabarcoding sites and you must quantify how OTU curation changes per-site taxonomic composition against survey ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-field-survey-validation))
- **metabarcoding-filter-parameterization**: Use when ASV occurrence tables produced by VTAM's 'filter' command on demultiplexed FASTQ data (e.g. COI MFZR/ZFZR or 16S shark reads) must have filtering thresholds optimized per dataset from control samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-filter-parameterization))
- **metabarcoding-filter-threshold-application**: Use when an ASV occurrence table (read counts per sample-replicate) contains low-frequency ASVs that closely match a much more abundant ASV and PCR/sequencing-error removal is needed before downstream filtering (replicates, chimeras, pseudogenes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-filter-threshold-application))
- **metabarcoding-motu-clustering**: Use when dereplicated, chimera-filtered metabarcoding reads (e.g. FASTA output of vsearch uchime-denovo) must be clustered into MOTU representative sequences before taxonomic assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-motu-clustering))
- **metabarcoding-occurrence-table-curation**: Use when you have a replicate-structured ASV-by-sample occurrence table (read counts Nijk per ASV i, sample j, replicate k) plus merged fasta sequences from VTAM merge/sortreads, and you need to remove chimeras and non-reproducible occurrences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-occurrence-table-curation))
- **metabarcoding-otu-table-manipulation**: Use when sample-by-taxon community tables from eDNA metabarcoding (e.g., COI and 16S arthropod OTUs from flower samples) are grouped by host plant species and the goal is to judge whether arthropod richness is saturated with current sampling effort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-otu-table-manipulation))
- **metabarcoding-parameter-tuning-and-fp-evaluation**: Use when calibrating metabarcoding pipeline parameters on ITS/16S/18S/COI amplicon data or validating recovery of known mock-community members via false positives and F1-scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-parameter-tuning-and-fp-evaluation))
- **metabarcoding-pipeline-architecture-design**: Use when building or extending a metabarcoding pipeline for 16S/18S/ITS/COI marker genes that must support tool/parameter exploration without re-running earlier stages, e.g. swapping OTU clustering or ASV inference methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-pipeline-architecture-design))
- **metabarcoding-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw amplicon FASTQ datasets with mock/negative controls and replicates must be processed through multiple metabarcoding pipelines (VTAM, DADA2+LULU, OBITools3+metabaR) under one reproducible setup for fair benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-pipeline-execution))
- **metabarcoding-pipeline-intermediate-file-handling**: Use when an OBITools-based eDNA metabarcoding workflow has produced filtered, aligned reads (e.g. post illuminapairedend, ngsfilter and obigrep 120–135 bp) and needs dereplication into a representative-sequence file consumed by vsearch/sumaclust steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-pipeline-intermediate-file-handling))
- **metabarcoding-read-count-normalization**: Use when you have an ASV occurrence table with read counts Nijk (ASV i, sample j, replicate k) from HTS amplicon data plus negative controls, and you need to remove low-frequency noise occurrences before downstream replicate-consistency or taxonomic-assignment steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metabarcoding-read-count-normalization))
